Motivation: 现有潜动作模型在动作抽象程度和生成保真度之间存在根本性权衡，通常需要两阶段训练或仅预测光流来规避该问题，缺乏统一的解决方案。

Method: 提出DiLA（解耦潜动作世界模型），通过内容-结构解耦实现动作学习与生成质量的协同优化：利用潜动作学习中的预测瓶颈驱动模型将空间布局蒸馏到结构路径，视觉细节分配到独立的内容路径进行生成。

Result: 在视频生成质量、动作迁移、视觉规划和流形可解释性等多个任务上取得了优越性能，证明了解耦与潜动作学习之间的协同进化关系。

Conclusion: DiLA作为一个统一框架，同时实现了高层动作抽象和高保真生成，推进了自监督世界模型学习的前沿。

Abstract: Latent Action Models (LAMs) enable the learning of world models from unlabeled video by inferring abstract actions between consecutive frames. However, LAMs face a fundamental trade-off between action abstraction and generation fidelity. Existing methods typically circumvent this issue by using two-stage training with pre-trained world models or by limiting predictions to optical flow. In this paper, we introduce DiLA, a novel Disentangled Latent Action world model that aims to resolve this trade-off via content-structure disentanglement. Our key insight is that disentanglement and latent action learning are co-evolving: the predictive bottleneck inherent in latent action learning serves as a driving force for disentanglement, compelling the model to distill spatial layouts into the structure pathway while offloading visual details to a separate content pathway for generation. This synergy yields a continuous, semantically structured latent action space without compromising generative quality. DiLA achieves superior results in video generation quality, action transfer, visual planning, and manifold interpretability. These findings establish DiLA as a unified framework that simultaneously achieves high-level action abstraction and high-fidelity generation, advancing the frontier of self-supervised world model learning.

</details>


### [38] [UAM: A Dual-Stream Perspective on Forgetting in VLA Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15735)
*Jianke Zhang,Yuanfei Luo,Yucheng Hu,Xiaoyu Chen,Yanjiang Guo,Ziyang Liu,Hongbin Xu,Tian Lan,Jianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 当前视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型在微调过程中会侵蚀视觉-语言模型(VLM)的多模态能力，作者称之为"具身税"。受生物视觉双流组织启发，提出统一动作模型(UAM)，通过添加并行"背侧专家"（类比大脑背侧通路）并初始化为生成模型、训练中预测视觉动态，在仅使用动作数据端到端训练下，保留VLM超过95%的多模态能力，同时取得最高的平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在微调时系统性地侵蚀了VLM的多模态能力，这种"具身税"源于结构瓶颈：单一编码器被迫同时支撑语言语义和控制相关视觉特征，而生物视觉将识别与视觉运动控制分属不同通路。

Method: 提出UAM，添加并行Dorsal Expert（类比大脑背侧通路），从预训练生成模型初始化Dorsal Expert，使用预测视觉动态的中级推理目标训练，整个VLA端到端在纯动作数据上训练，无需参数冻结、梯度停止或辅助VL共训练。

Result: UAM保留了VLM超过95%的多模态能力，同时在多种操控任务（包括未见物体、新物体-目标组合、指令变化）中达到基线中最高的平均成功率。

Conclusion: 语义保留可以通过架构分离本身实现，而非依赖冻结权重或辅助数据回放；保留的语义能力能自然地从VLM迁移到动作中的语义泛化。

Abstract: Vision--language--action (VLA) models are typically built by fine-tuning a pretrained vision--language model (VLM) on action data. However, we show that this standard recipe systematically erodes the VLM's multimodal competence, a side effect we call the embodiment tax. But do VLAs have to forget? Inspired by the two-stream organization of biological vision, we trace this degradation to a structural bottleneck: current VLAs ask a single encoder to support both language-grounded semantics and control-relevant visual features, whereas biological vision separates recognition and visuomotor control into distinct pathways. Building on this view, we propose the Unified Action Model (UAM), which adds a parallel Dorsal Expert, an analog of the brain's dorsal pathway. To make the Dorsal Expert an effective second pathway and reduce the control-learning burden on the VLM, we initialize it from a pretrained generative model and train it with a mid-level reasoning objective that predicts visual dynamics. This design allows us to train the whole VLA end-to-end on action data alone: with no parameter freezing, no gradient stopping, and no auxiliary VL co-training, UAM retains over $95\%$ of the underlying VLM's multimodal capability and at the same time achieves the highest average success rate among baselines on a variety of manipulation tasks that probe out-of-distribution generalization, including unseen objects, novel object--target compositions, and instruction variation. Together, these results suggest that semantic preservation in VLAs can emerge from architectural separation itself, rather than being enforced by frozen weights or auxiliary data replay, and that this preserved semantic capability can naturally transfer from VLMs to semantic generalization in actions.

</details>


### [39] [BiomedAP: A Vision-Informed Dual-Anchor Framework with Gated Cross-Modal Fusion for Robust Medical Vision-Language Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15736)
*Huanyang Tong,Kai Liu,Fangjun Kuang,Huiling Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BiomedAP，一种视觉引导的双锚点框架，通过门控跨模态融合和双锚点约束机制，解决生物医学视觉-语言模型在少样本诊断中对提示变异的脆弱性问题，在11个基准上取得优异结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物医学VLM在少样本诊断中严重依赖理想化的"黄金提示"，但临床场景下的描述通常包含噪声且高度异构，现有的模态独立优化策略导致跨模态对齐不稳定，亟需一种鲁棒的提示学习框架。

Method: BiomedAP包含两个核心机制：(1) 门控跨模态融合，实现逐层模态交互，作为动态噪声调节器抑制无关文本线索；(2) 双锚点约束，将可学习提示正则化到由专家模板（高锚点）和少样本视觉原型（低锚点）导出的稳定语义质心。

Result: 在11个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，BiomedAP持续超越基线方法，在少样本准确率上具有竞争力，且在提示扰动下表现出显著增强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: BiomedAP通过视觉引导的双锚点框架有效解决了生物医学VLM对提示变异的脆弱性问题，为临床场景下的鲁棒少样本诊断提供了新思路。

Abstract: Biomedical Vision--Language Models (VLMs) have shown remarkable promise in few-shot medical diagnosis but face a critical bottleneck: \textit{fragility to prompt variations}.Existing adaptation frameworks typically optimize visual and textual prompts as independent streams, relying on ideal ``Golden Prompts''. In clinical reality, where descriptions are often noisy and heterogeneous, this modality isolation leads to unstable cross-modal alignment.
  To address this, we propose BiomedAP, a vision-informed dual-anchor framework with gated cross-modal fusion.BiomedAP enforces synergistic alignment through two mechanisms: (1) Gated Cross-Modal Fusion, which enables layer-wise interaction between modalities, acting as a dynamic noise regulator to suppress irrelevant textual cues; and (2) a Dual-Anchor Constraint that regularizes learnable prompts toward stable semantic centroids derived from both expert templates (High Anchors) and few-shot visual prototypes (Low Anchors).
  Extensive experiments across 11 benchmarks demonstrate that BiomedAP consistently surpasses baselines, achieving competitive few-shot accuracy and markedly enhanced robustness under prompt perturbations.
  Our code is available at: https://github.com/tongdiedie/BiomedAP.
  Keywords: Vision-Language Models; Prompt Learning; Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning; Few-shot Learning

</details>


### [40] [BARRIER: Bounded Activation Regions for Robust Information Erasure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15737)
*Jan Miksa,Patryk Krukowski,Przemysław Spurek,Dawid Damian Rymarczyk,Marcin Sendera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BARRIER提出了一种基于激活空间几何结构的机器遗忘框架，通过区间算术和SVD投影将遗忘区域约束在超立方体内，同时数学上保证保留分布不受影响，实现了高精度概念擦除与最小附带损伤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于权重的机器遗忘方法在擦除目标概念时缺乏对中性概念保留的形式化数学保证，导致显著的附带损伤，迫使算法只能采用保守更新，限制了遗忘效果。

Method: 提出BARRIER框架，将干预点从静态模型权重转移到隐层激活的动态几何结构。利用SVD分解对激活空间进行投影，结合区间算术(IA)将目标区域封装在边界超立方体中，仅在遗忘区间内执行遗忘更新，同时对补集区域的模型响应进行数学上可证明的边界约束。

Result: 在分类器和扩散模型上均达到最先进水平，实现了目标概念擦除的最大化，同时保障了其他所有表征的完整性。关键在于，这种几何构造使得在遗忘区域内可以进行高度激进的遗忘更新而不会损害保留分布。

Conclusion: BARRIER将知识保留从经验启发式转变为具有概率尾界的形式化优化目标，从根本上解决了机器遗忘中擦除精度与知识保留之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Machine unlearning has reached a critical bottleneck. As traditional weight-space interventions focus primarily on erasing targeted concepts, they often fail to prevent the unintended suppression of other significant representations. This leads to substantial collateral damage, with essential knowledge being forgotten, because these methods lack formal mathematical guarantees for the preservation of neutral concepts. To avoid degradation, they are frequently forced into conservative updates. We propose BARRIER (Bounded Activation Regions for Robust Information Erasure), a paradigm-shifting framework that shifts the locus of intervention from static model weights to the dynamic geometry of hidden-layer activations. Unlike existing methods, BARRIER employs Interval Arithmetic (IA) on SVD-based projections of the activation space to encapsulate the specific target region within a bounding hypercube. By driving unlearning updates exclusively within this forget interval and mathematically bounding the model response on the complement, we ensure rigorous protection of the retain distribution. This geometric construction transforms the preservation of knowledge from an empirical heuristic into a formal optimization target with a probabilistic tail bound on functional drift. Crucially, this stability permits highly aggressive unlearning updates within the forget region. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that BARRIER matches state-of-the-art trade-offs across classifiers and diffusion models, maximizing targeted concept erasure while safeguarding the integrity of all other representations. Our code is available at https://github.com/OneAndZero24/BARRIER.

</details>


### [41] [HyperDiT: Hyper-Connected Transformers for High-Fidelity Pixel-Space Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15741)
*Yu He,Lichen Ma,Zipeng Guo,Xinyuan Shan,Jingling Fu,Dong Chen,Junshi Huang,Yan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HyperDiT提出超连接跨尺度交互框架，通过交叉注意力、尺度感知旋转位置编码和视觉基础模型寄存器，解决像素空间扩散模型中全局语义与局部细节的粒度困境，在ImageNet 256×256上实现1.56 FID的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素空间扩散模型面临"粒度困境"：捕获全局语义需要大patch尺度，而生成高保真细节需要细粒度输入。现有方法难以同时兼顾两者。

Method: 提出HyperDiT统一框架：(1) 使用Cross-Attention替代AdaLN，让细粒度token查询多层级语义锚点；(2) 引入尺度感知旋转位置编码(SA-RoPE)解决多尺度交互中的空间错位；(3) 利用预训练视觉基础模型(VFM)的Registers学习稠密语义，减少生成幻觉和伪影。

Result: 在ImageNet 256×256上达到SOTA FID 1.56，在像素空间中直接生成。

Conclusion: HyperDiT通过细粒度流与语义引导相结合，为高保真像素生成提供了优越的范式。

Abstract: Pixel-space diffusion models bypass the reconstruction bottleneck of Variational Autoencoders (VAEs) but face a fundamental "granularity dilemma": capturing global semantics favors large patch scales, while generating high-fidelity details demands fine-grained inputs. To address this issue, we propose HyperDiT, a unified framework establishing Hyper-Connected Cross-Scale Interactions to bridge the semantic and pixel manifold. Diverging from injecting semantics by AdaLN, HyperDiT utilizes Cross-Attention mechanisms, enabling fine-grained tokens to query multi-level semantic anchors globally. To resolve the spatial mismatch during multi-scale interactions, we introduce Scale-Aware Rotary Position Embedding (SA-RoPE) to ensure precise geometric alignment among tokens of varying patch sizes. Furthermore, we incorporate Registers to learn the dense semantics from a pretrained Visual Foundation Model (VFM), effectively reducing generation hallucination and artifacts. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HyperDiT achieves state-of-the-art (SoTA) FID of $\mathbf{1.56}$ on ImageNet $256\times256$ directly within the pixel space. By combining the fine-grained stream with semantic guidance, HyperDiT offers a superior paradigm for high-fidelity pixel generation.

</details>


### [42] [Attribute-Grounded Selective Reasoning for Artwork Emotion Understanding with Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15755)
*Cheng Zhang,Yuer Liu,Zhiyu Zhou,Hongxia Xie,Wen-Huang Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态大模型在 artworks 情感理解中的属性泛滥问题，提出了属性引导的选择性推理（AGSR）框架，扩展了 EmoArt 数据集（增加 1,400 幅人工标注的情感显著性数据），并设计了 FAB-G 多智能体推理方法，通过预测属性级显著性并约束下游情感分析，在情感、唤醒度、效价预测及解释紧凑性上均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLMs 虽然能生成流畅的艺术作品情感解释，但存在属性泛滥问题——模型列举了大量可见的形式属性，却无法区分哪些属性真正支撑了情感判断。因此需要建立一种机制，让模型仅关注对情感有实际贡献的属性，而非罗列所有存在属性。

Method: 1）形式化 Attribute-Grounded Selective Reasoning (AGSR) 任务，以预定义形式属性为证据单元，仅保留情感上可操作的属性进入解释；2）扩展 EmoArt 数据集，新增 1,400 幅由 15 位艺术训练标注者标注的人工显著性扩展，提供实例级监督；3）提出 FAB-G (Formal-Attribute Bottleneck-Guided reasoning) 监督式多智能体框架，先预测属性级显著性，再约束下游情感分析仅使用保留的线索。

Result: FAB-G 在情感、唤醒度、效价预测上取得一致提升；在 Dice 和 Tversky 指标上与人工标注的显著属性达成更强一致性；生成的解释比基于提示的基线方法更紧凑；跨数据集评估表明属性级显著性选择可迁移到 EmoArt 源分布之外的数据。

Conclusion: 属性级瓶颈引导的情感推理能有效缓解属性泛滥问题，提升情感理解的精确性和解释的紧凑性，且具备跨数据集泛化能力，同时也揭示了属性特定的边界情况。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can produce fluent artwork emotion explanations, but they often suffer from attribute flooding: they enumerate many visible formal attributes without identifying which cues actually support the affective judgment. We therefore formulate artwork emotion understanding as Attribute-Grounded Selective Reasoning (AGSR), where predefined formal attributes serve as evidence units and only emotionally operative attributes should enter the final interpretation. To make this problem measurable, we extend EmoArt, originally introduced at ACM MM 2025 as a 132,664-artwork resource with content, formal-attribute, valence-arousal, and emotion annotations, by adding a 1,400-artwork human salience extension annotated by 15 art-trained annotators. This extension provides instance-level supervision for distinguishing attributes that are merely present from those that are emotionally salient. We further propose FAB-G (Formal-Attribute Bottleneck-Guided reasoning), a supervised multi-agent framework that first predicts attribute-level salience and then constrains downstream emotional analysis to the retained cues. Experiments show that FAB-G yields consistent gains in emotion, arousal, and valence prediction, achieves stronger agreement with human-marked salient attributes under Dice and Tversky metrics, and produces substantially more compact final explanations than prompting-based baselines. Cross-dataset evaluation further suggests that attribute-grounded salience selection transfers beyond the source distribution of EmoArt, while also revealing attribute-specific boundary cases. The dataset and project page are available at https://zhiliangzhang.github.io/EmoArt-130k/

</details>


### [43] [Learn2Splat: Extending the Horizon of Learned 3DGS Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15760)
*Naama Pearl,Stefano Esposito,Haofei Xu,Amit Peleg,Patricia Gschossmann,Lorenzo Porzi,Peter Kontschieder,Gerard Pons-Moll,Andreas Geiger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对3D高斯泼溅优化的学习型优化器（Learned Optimizer），通过元学习方案（包括检查点缓冲区和优化器展开策略）扩展优化视野，在不依赖辅助机制的情况下避免长时间优化退化，实现了更快的收敛速度和零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准优化器（Adam、SGD）在3DGS优化中是通用的，无法捕捉场景中参数间的结构和空间关系，导致独立参数更新、优化效率低下且收敛缓慢。现有学习型优化器虽能预测相关更新，但受限于固定优化迭代次数且依赖手动调度学习率以避免退化。

Method: 提出元学习方案，包含检查点缓冲区（checkpoint buffer）和优化器展开策略（rollout strategy），扩展优化视野；架构上编码梯度尺度信息到潜在状态中，使优化器在长时间优化中不退化。

Result: 在早期新视角合成质量上显著提升，同时在长时间优化中保持稳定，并展现了零样本泛化到未见过的重建设置的能力。

Conclusion: 学习型优化器可以在无辅助机制下避免长时间优化退化，为3DGS优化提供更高效、鲁棒的方案，并建立了首个统一框架用于训练和评估学习型与传统优化器。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) optimization is most commonly performed using standard optimizers (Adam, SGD). While stable across diverse scenes, standard optimizers are general-purpose and not tailored to the structure of the problem. In particular, they produce independent parameter updates that do not capture the structural and spatial relationships within a scene, leading to inefficient optimization and slow convergence. Recent works introduced learned optimizers that predict correlated updates informed by inter-parameter and inter-Gaussian dependencies. However, these methods are trained for a fixed number of optimization iterations and rely on manually scheduled learning rates to avoid degradation. In this paper, we introduce a learned optimizer for 3DGS that avoids degradation over extended optimization horizons without auxiliary mechanisms. To enable this, we propose a meta-learning scheme that extends the optimization horizon via a checkpoint buffer and an optimizer rollout strategy, combined with an architecture that encodes gradient scale information in its latent states. Results show improved early novel view synthesis quality while remaining stable over long horizons, with zero-shot generalization to unseen reconstruction settings. To support our findings, we introduce the first unified framework for training and evaluating both learned and conventional optimizers across sparse and dense view settings. Code and models will be released publicly. Our project page is available at https://naamapearl.github.io/learn2splat .

</details>


### [44] [GRASP: Learning to Ground Social Reasoning in Multi-Person Non-Verbal Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15764)
*Junho Kim,Xu Cao,Houze Yang,Bikram Boote,Ana Jojic,Fiona Ryan,Bolin Lai,Sangmin Lee,James M. Rehg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GRASP数据集（含29万问答对、46K视频、749小时），通过细粒度注视和指示手势事件连接高层社交问答，并提出Social Grounding Reward（SGR）训练信号，提升多模态大模型在多人物视频中社交推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大模型在多人物视频中难以识别谁与谁进行社交互动，缺乏连接细粒度非语言线索（注视、手势）与高层社交推理的数据集和方法。

Method: 构建GRASP数据集（16类别分类法，覆盖注视、手势及联合注视-手势推理），提出Social Grounding Reward（SGR）学习信号，利用身份一致的注视轨迹、指示手势及其联合社交事件来引导模型推理互动参与者。

Result: SGR在GRASP-Bench上提升性能，同时在相关社交视频QA基准上保持零样本性能。

Conclusion: 通过细粒度社交事件监督信号可以提升多模态大模型在多人物场景下的社交交互推理能力，且不影响其在其他任务上的泛化表现。

Abstract: Understanding social interactions requires reasoning over subtle non-verbal cues, yet current multimodal large language models (MLLMs) often fail to identify who interacts with whom in multi-person videos. We introduce GRASP, a large-scale social reasoning dataset that connects high-level social QA with fine-grained gaze and deictic gesture events. GRASP contains 290K question--answer pairs over 46K videos totaling 749 hours, organized by a 16-category taxonomy spanning gaze, gesture, and joint gaze--gesture reasoning, together with GRASP-Bench for evaluation. Unlike prior resources that focus on either isolated cues or high-level social QA, GRASP builds questions from identity-consistent gaze trajectories, deictic gestures, and their joint compositions into social events. Moreover, we propose Social Grounding Reward (SGR), a learning signal that uses these social events to encourage models to reason about the participants involved in each interaction. Experiments show that SGR improves performance on GRASP-Bench while maintaining zero-shot performance on related social video QA benchmarks.

</details>


### [45] [Reversing the Flow: Generation-to-Understanding Synergy in Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15792)
*Yujun Tong,Dongliang Chang,Zijin Yin,Xintong Liu,Yuanchen Fang,Zhanyu Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出生成到理解（G2U）协同框架，将视觉生成作为显式的中间推理步骤，使模型能够执行受控生成操作（如细节增强、上下文扩展、结构可视化）产生自生成的视觉思维，并反馈回模型以优化感知，无需重新训练或外部工具。在12个基准测试上，这种反向信息流持续改进了多模态理解，同时揭示了生成保真度与感知增益的界限关系，以及当前大模型缺乏真正自我反思能力的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型中，视觉理解指导生成是单向的（理解→生成），生成如何支持理解（生成→理解）很少被研究。作者希望打破这种不对称性，探索视觉生成作为中间推理步骤来增强多模态理解的可能性。

Method: 提出G2U框架，模型先执行受控生成操作（细节增强、上下文扩展、结构可视化），产生自生成的视觉思维（self-generated visual thoughts），然后将这些生成结果反馈回模型以优化感知。整个过程无需重新训练或外部工具，实现了生成→理解的反向信息流。

Result: 在12个多模态基准测试上，G2U框架持续提升了多模态理解性能。实验发现：(1) 生成保真度与感知增益之间存在明确的界限关系；(2) 不同类型的编辑提示（edit prompts）对迁移效率有不同影响； (3) 模型能产生合理的编辑，但自生成的视觉思维缺乏稳定的任务对齐，说明当前大模型缺乏真正的自我反思能力。

Conclusion: 当前统一多模态模型的认知机制存在缺失——生成并非理解的终点，而是理解的起点。这项工作揭示了生成→理解方向的重要价值，并为未来构建真正双向协同的统一多模态AI提供了方向性指导。

Abstract: The long-standing goal of multimodal AI is to build unified models in which visual understanding and visual generation mutually enhance one another. Despite recent works such as BAGEL, BLIP3o achieves remarkable progress; In practice, however, this unification remains one-directional: understanding routinely guides generation, yet how and why generation can support understanding is rarely investigated. We revisit this asymmetry and propose Generation-to-Understanding (G2U) synergy, where visual generation becomes an explicit intermediate reasoning step. Our framework enables a model to perform controlled generative acts, such as detail enhancement, context expansion or structural visualisation, to produce self-generated visual thoughts, which are then fed back into the model to refine perception without retraining or external tools. Through a comprehensive evaluation on twelve benchmarks, this reversed information flow consistently improves multimodal understanding. We show that generative fidelity bounds perceptual gain and that distinct families of edit prompts govern transfer efficiency. We further analyse whether models can decide what to imagine. While they can produce plausible edits, these self-generated visual thoughts lack stable task alignment, revealing that current large multimodal models fall short of true self-reflection. This work exposes a missing mechanism in unified cognition and suggests that imagination is not the end of understanding but its beginning.

</details>


### [46] [Cross-Modal Registration Between 3D and 2D Fingerprints via Pose-Aware Unwrapping and Point-Cloud Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15796)
*Xiongjun Guan,Jianjiang Feng,Jie Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种统一的3D指纹预处理与配准框架，包含非参数化展开、点云融合、椭圆姿态归一化和跨模态配准四个模块，实现了3D指纹与接触式/非接触式2D指纹的高效跨模态匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D指纹虽能保留全局手指几何和局部脊线结构并避免接触变形，但难以与传统的2D指纹系统集成，亟需一个连接3D采集与跨模态匹配的中间处理框架。

Method: 1) 非参数化可视化与展开方法，将3D点云转换为卷展等效2D表示；2) 点云融合流水线，配准拼接多个局部3D采集；3) 基于椭圆的姿态归一化方法；4) 姿态感知的跨模态配准策略。

Result: 在150指自建多模态数据集上，3D融合误差约0.09mm，非接触式2D-3D配准达到脊级投影精度，姿态感知展开提升了真实匹配分数。

Conclusion: 该框架使3D指纹可作为跨异构指纹模态的有效几何桥接，支持3D指纹在混合指纹识别系统中的实用化。

Abstract: Three-dimensional (3D) fingerprints preserve global finger geometry and local ridge structure while avoiding contact-induced deformation, but they remain difficult to integrate with legacy two-dimensional (2D) fingerprint systems. This paper addresses the intermediate stage between 3D acquisition and cross-modal matching, and presents a unified framework for 3D fingerprint preprocessing and registration across contactless and contact-based 2D modalities. The framework combines four components: 1) a nonparametric visualization and unwrapping method that converts a 3D fingerprint point cloud into a rolled-equivalent 2D representation without relying on a global finger-shape model; 2) a point-cloud fusion pipeline that registers and mosaics multiple partial 3D captures into a more complete fingerprint model; 3) an ellipse-based pose normalization method for canonical finger alignment; and 4) a pose-aware cross-modal registration strategy that improves compatibility between 3D fingerprints and both contactless and contact-based 2D fingerprints. Experiments on a self-collected multimodal fingerprint database containing 150 fingers show that the proposed framework achieves ridge-level 3D registration accuracy, robust pose estimation, and consistent gains in 2D compatibility. In particular, the 3D fusion error is concentrated around 0.09 mm, contactless 2D--3D registration reaches ridge-scale projection accuracy, and pose-aware unwrapping improves genuine matching scores relative to generic 3D unwrapping. These results support the use of 3D fingerprints as an effective geometric bridge across heterogeneous fingerprint modalities.

</details>


### [47] [MultiMat: Multimodal Program Synthesis for Procedural Materials using Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.22151)
*Jonas Belouadi,Tamy Boubekeur,Adrien Kaiser*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MultiMat，一种多模态程序合成框架，利用大型多模态模型同时处理视觉和文本图表示，以改进程序化材质图的生成。该方法在包含生产质量程序化材质的新数据集上训练，并结合约束树搜索推理算法，在无条件和条件图合成任务中均取得了优于纯文本基线的结果，实现了新的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序化材质节点图的创建通常需要专业训练，现有神经程序合成方法仅将图表示为文本程序，未能捕捉节点图固有的视觉空间特性，而这正是人类易于理解的关键。因此，需要一种能同时利用视觉和文本信息的多模态方法。

Method: 提出MultiMat多模态程序合成框架：(1) 利用大型多模态模型同时处理节点图的视觉渲染表示和文本程序表示；(2) 构建生产质量程序化材质的新数据集进行训练；(3) 设计约束树搜索推理算法，在保证静态正确性的同时高效导航程序空间。

Result: 实验表明，多模态程序合成方法在无条件和条件图合成中均比纯文本基线更高效，生成的视觉质量和保真度更高，建立了新的最优性能水平。

Conclusion: 多模态程序合成方法能更有效地生成程序化材质节点图，证明了融合视觉和文本模态对提升生成质量和效率的重要性，为程序化材质设计领域提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Material node graphs are programs that generate the 2D channels of procedural materials, including geometry such as roughness and displacement maps, and reflectance such as albedo and conductivity maps. They are essential in computer graphics for representing the appearance of virtual 3D objects parametrically and at arbitrary resolution. In particular, their directed acyclic graph structure and intermediate states enable a modular, interpretable workflow for interactive appearance modeling. However, creating such graphs remains challenging and typically requires professional training. While recent neural program synthesis approaches attempt to simplify this process, they solely represent graphs as textual programs, failing to capture the inherently visual-spatial nature of node graphs that makes them accessible to humans. To address this gap, we present MultiMat, a multimodal program synthesis framework that leverages large multimodal models to process both visual and textual graph representations for improved generation of procedural material graphs. We train our models on a new dataset of production-quality procedural materials and combine them with a constrained tree search inference algorithm that ensures static correctness while efficiently navigating the program space. Our experimental results show that our multimodal program synthesis method is more efficient in both unconditional and conditional graph synthesis with higher visual quality and fidelity than text-only baselines, establishing new state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [48] [Embedding-perturbed Exploration Preference Optimization for Flow Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15803)
*Sujie Hu,Chubin Chen,Jiashu Zhu,Jiahong Wu,Xiangxiang Chu,Xiu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出嵌入扰动探索偏好优化（E²PO）框架，通过结构化嵌入级扰动维持组内方差，解决GRPO等基于群体优化方法中因组内方差消失导致的训练不稳定、策略停滞或奖励黑客问题，显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO等群体优化框架存在组内方差快速衰减的根本缺陷：样本间独特性随训练下降，方差趋近于零，导致学习信号消失、训练不稳定、策略过早停滞或奖励黑客。现有策略（改变初始噪声、增大组规模等）无法解决这一根本问题。

Method: 提出E²PO框架，在样本组内引入结构化的嵌入级扰动，确保训练过程中保持稳健的方差，从而维持可区分性信号，保障优化的持续进行。

Result: 大量实验表明，E²PO显著优于现有基线方法，实现了与人类偏好更忠实的对齐。

Conclusion: E²PO通过嵌入级扰动有效解决了群体优化中方差消失的关键问题，为生成模型与人类偏好对齐提供了一种更稳定、更有效的优化范式。

Abstract: Recent advancements have established Reinforcement Learning (RL) as a pivotal paradigm for aligning generative models with human intent. However, group-based optimization frameworks (e.g., GRPO) face a critical limitation: the rapid decay of intra-group variance. As the distinctiveness among samples within a group diminishes, the variance approaches zero. This eliminates the very learning signal required for optimization, rendering the process unstable and forcing the policy into premature stagnation or reward hacking. Existing strategies, such as varying the initial noise or increasing group sizes, often fail to address this fundamental issue, resulting in training instability or diminishing returns. To overcome these challenges, we propose $\textbf{Embedding-perturbed Exploration Preference Optimization (}E^2\textbf{PO)}$, a novel framework that sustains optimization through embedding-level perturbation. Our method introduces structured, embedding-level perturbations within sample groups, guaranteeing a robust variance that preserves the discriminative signal throughout the training process. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving a more faithful alignment with human preference.

</details>


### [49] [FashionChameleon: Towards Real-Time and Interactive Human-Garment Video Customization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15824)
*Quanjian Song,Yefeng Shen,Mengting Chen,Hao Sun,Jinsong Lan,Xiaoyong Zhu,Bo Zheng,Liujuan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FashionChameleon 是一个实时交互式框架，支持用户在多服装视频定制中动态切换服装，同时保持运动连贯性。它通过上下文学习训练教师模型、流式蒸馏加速生成、以及免训练的KV缓存重调度实现多服装切换，在单GPU上达到23.8 FPS，比现有方法快30-180倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人体视频定制方法无法支持低延迟、交互式的服装控制，这在电商和内容创作等应用中至关重要。本文旨在实现仅使用单服装视频数据即可完成交互式多服装视频定制，同时保持运动连贯性。

Method: (i) 教师模型：基于上下文学习在单参考服装-图像对上训练，保留图像到视频的训练范式，通过强制参考与服装图像不匹配来隐式学习单服装切换时的连贯性。(ii) 流式蒸馏：结合上下文学习的教师强制微调，并通过梯度重加权分布匹配蒸馏提升外推一致性。(iii) 免训练KV缓存重调度：包括服装KV刷新、历史KV收回和参考KV解耦，实现服装切换同时保持运动连贯。

Result: FashionChameleon 支持交互式定制和一致的长视频外推，在单GPU上实现23.8 FPS的实时生成，比现有基线快30-180倍。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种创新的实时交互式多服装视频定制框架，通过上下文学习、流式蒸馏和KV缓存重调度三大技术，在保证运动连贯性的同时大幅提升生成速度，为电商和内容创作等应用提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Human-centric video customization, particularly at the garment level, has shown significant commercial value. However, existing approaches cannot support low-latency and interactive garment control, which is crucial for applications such as e-commerce and content creation. This paper studies how to achieve interactive multi-garment video customization while preserving motion coherence using only single-garment video data. We present FashionChameleon, a real-time and interactive framework for human-garment customization in autoregressive video generation, where users can interactively switch garment during generation. FashionChameleon consists of three key techniques: (i) Instead of training on multi-garment video data, we train a Teacher Model with In-Context Learning on a single reference-garment pair. By retaining the image-to-video training paradigm while enforcing a mismatch between the reference and garment image, the model is encouraged to implicitly preserve coherence during single-garment switching. (ii) To achieve consistency and efficiency during generation, we introduce Streaming Distillation with In-Context Learning, which fine-tunes the model with in-context teacher forcing and improves extrapolation consistency via gradient-reweighted distribution matching distillation. (iii) To extend the model for interactive multi-garment video customization, we propose Training-Free KV Cache Rescheduling, which includes garment KV refresh, historical KV withdraw, and reference KV disentangle to achieve garment switching while preserving motion coherence. Our FashionChameleon uniquely supports interactive customization and consistent long-video extrapolation, while achieving real-time generation at 23.8 FPS on a single GPU, 30-180$\times$ faster than existing baselines.

</details>


### [50] [Community-aware evaluation and threshold calibration for open-set plankton image recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15835)
*Xi Chen,Eryuan Huang,Yingjun Xiao,Gang Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出自动 plankton 图像识别中的开集识别方法通常用样本级指标（如 AUROC、AUPR）评估，但生态监测依赖群落级的物种丰度和多样性估计。作者通过三个数据集（海洋浮游动物、海洋浮游植物、淡水浮游生物）定义了一种 Bray-Curtis 风格的开集群落失真度量（OSCD），发现样本级 OOD 指标不足以选择生态操作点，而群落感知的阈值校准能降低 OSCD，但效果依赖于验证群落的代表性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开集识别评估方法使用样本级指标（如 AUROC、AUPR），但这些指标与生态监测实际所需的群落级丰度和多样性估计存在严重不匹配，导致实际部署时生态测量质量无法保证。

Method: 定义了开集群落失真（OSCD）指标（Bray-Curtis 风格，包含已知类加未知箱，有方向分量区分高估/低估），在三个数据集（SYKE-ZooScan 2024, SYKE-IFCB 2022, ZooLake）上进行实验，比较样本级指标与群落级指标的表现，并引入群落感知的阈值校准方法。

Result: 闭集分类器已知类准确率高，但未知样本常被高置信度且结构化地吸收；样本级 OOD 指标不足以选择生态操作点；FPR@95% unknown-recall 阈值导致较大的 OSCD；群落感知阈值校准在部分数据集上降低了 OSCD，但在 ZooLake 上优势不明显，最佳方法为原型距离变体而非 MSP。

Conclusion: 开集 plankton 识别应作为生态测量问题而非仅作为样本级检测任务来评估，群落感知校准的收益取决于验证群落代表性与固定召回率与群落最优值之间的差距。

Abstract: Automated plankton image recognition is increasingly used in aquatic ecosystem monitoring, but deployed classifiers inevitably encounter unseen taxa and non-target particles. Open-set recognition methods are usually evaluated with sample-level metrics such as AUROC, AUPR, and FPR@95% unknown-recall operating points, whereas ecological monitoring depends on community-level estimates of taxon abundance and diversity. This study examines the mismatch between these objectives using controlled pseudo-communities and three datasets spanning marine zooplankton imaged by ZooScan, marine phytoplankton imaged by IFCB, and freshwater plankton imaged by an in-situ camera. We define Open-Set Community Distortion (OSCD), a Bray-Curtis-style error over known taxa plus an unknown bin, with directional components distinguishing known-taxon overestimation from underestimation. Closed-set classifiers achieved high known-class accuracy, but unknown samples were often absorbed with high confidence and in structured ways. Sample-level OOD metrics were not sufficient to select ecological operating points: for MSP, FPR@95% unknown-recall thresholds produced large test-community OSCD on all three datasets mainly because true known taxa were over-rejected into the unknown bin. Community-aware threshold calibration reduced MSP OSCD relative to fixed 95% known recall on SYKE-ZooScan 2024 and SYKE-IFCB 2022; on ZooLake the fixed-recall baseline was already close to the community-aware threshold, and the best community-level method was a prototype-distance variant rather than MSP. The benefit of community-aware calibration therefore depends on validation-community representativeness and the gap between fixed recall and the community optimum. These results show that open-set plankton recognition should be evaluated as an ecological measurement problem, not only as a sample-level detection task.

</details>


### [51] [Are VLMs Seeing or Just Saying? Uncovering the Illusion of Visual Re-examination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15864)
*Chufan Shi,Cheng Yang,Yaokang Wu,Linhao Jin,Bo Shui,Taylor Berg-Kirkpatrick,Xuezhe Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过VisualSwap图像交换探测框架，发现VLMs在推理中产生的自我反思语句（如“让我再看一下图片”）并非真正的视觉重新检查，而是习得的文本模式。模型在图像被替换后几乎无法察觉，且思考型模型比指令型模型脆弱近3倍，扩展规模也无济于事。注意力分析表明，用户指令能提升视觉注意力，而自我反思不能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究VLMs在推理过程中产生的自我反思性语句（如“让我再检查一下图片”）是否真正触发了视觉重新检查，还是仅仅是一种习得的文本模式。现有研究缺乏对此类自我反思行为真实性的系统性评估。

Method: 提出VisualSwap图像交换探测框架：模型对一张图像进行推理后，将其替换为视觉相似但语义不同的图像，测试模型是否能察觉到变化。构建了包含800个图像对的VS-Bench基准，在Qwen3-VL、Kimi-VL、ERNIE-VL等模型上进行实验，并结合注意力分析探究机制。

Result: 模型在图像被替换后几乎无法察觉，准确率下降高达60%。思考型模型比指令型模型脆弱近3倍，且扩展模型规模无法缓解问题。用户指令显著提升对视觉token的注意力，但自我反思语句不能。

Conclusion: 当前VLMs在声称进行视觉重新检查时，更多是“说”而非真正“看”。自我反思语句并未触发真实的视觉重检，模型缺乏真正的视觉基础能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) often produce self-reflective statements like "let me check the figure again" during reasoning. Do such statements trigger genuine visual re-examination, or are they merely learned textual patterns? We investigate this via VisualSwap, an image-swap probing framework: after a model reasons over an image, we replace it with a visually similar but semantically different one and test whether the model notices. We introduce VS-Bench, 800 image pairs curated from MathVista, MathVerse, MathVision, and MMMU-Pro. Experiments on Qwen3-VL, Kimi-VL, and ERNIE-VL reveal a striking failure: models overwhelmingly miss the swap, with accuracy dropping by up to 60%. Counterintuitively, thinking models are nearly 3x more vulnerable than their instructed counterparts, and scaling offers no mitigation. Multi-turn user instructions restore visual grounding, but self-generated reflective statements during continuous generation do not. Attention analysis explains why: user instructions substantially elevate attention to visual tokens, whereas self-reflection does not. Current VLMs tend to say rather than actually see when claiming to perform visual re-examination. Our code and dataset are available at the project page: https://visualswap.github.io

</details>


### [52] [WorldAct: Activating Monolithic 3D Worlds into Interactive-Ready Object-Centric Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15843)
*Jichen Hu,Jiawei Guo,Jiazhong Cen,Chen Yang,Sikuang Li,Wei Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldAct是一个将静态生成3D世界转换为可编辑、可交互场景的框架，通过多模态智能体引导场景分解、识别可操作物体、重建几何对齐的物体级网格，并修复残差背景，支持物体级编辑、碰撞感知操作和具身任务执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D场景生成系统（如Marble）能生成连贯可探索的3D环境，但输出通常是静态的整体资产，缺乏可编辑性和物理交互能力，限制了其在沉浸式内容创作和具身仿真中的应用。

Method: 使用多模态智能体引导场景分解，识别可操作物体，重建几何对齐的物体级网格以支持交互，并通过3D修复技术恢复残差背景。

Result: 生成的场景支持物体级编辑、碰撞感知操作和具身任务执行，同时保持全局场景连贯性。实验表明WorldAct能够实现比原始生成场景更丰富的交互场景。

Conclusion: WorldAct提供了一条将静态生成3D世界转化为可编辑、可交互3D世界模型的实际路径，有望推动沉浸式内容创作和具身仿真领域的发展。

Abstract: Recent 3D world modeling systems based on generative scene synthesis, such as Marble, can create coherent and explorable 3D environments, yet their outputs are typically static monolithic assets with limited editability and physical interaction. This restricts their use in immersive content creation and embodied simulation, where generated worlds must be actively modified and manipulated. To tackle this challenge, we present WorldAct, a framework that converts static generated 3D worlds into editable and interaction-ready scenes. WorldAct uses a multimodal agent to guide scene decomposition, identify actionable objects, reconstruct geometrically aligned object-level meshes for interaction, and restore the residual background via 3D inpainting. The resulting scenes support object-level editing, collision-aware manipulation, and embodied task execution while preserving global scene coherence. Experiments show that WorldAct enables richer interaction scenarios than the original generated scenes, suggesting a practical path toward editable and interactive 3D world models.

</details>


### [53] [GHOST: Geometry-Hierarchical Online Streaming Token Eviction for Efficient 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15852)
*Leyang Chen,Junyi Wu,Zhiteng Li,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GHOST是一个无需训练的KV缓存管理框架，利用模型自身的3D几何输出在线剔除冗余token，将KV缓存削减近一半，同时实现1.75倍加速推理，并保持优秀的重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从长单目视频序列进行流式3D重建时，KV缓存随序列长度线性增长，造成严重内存瓶颈。现有方法要么将缓存截断为固定锚帧导致重建质量下降，要么依赖与3D场景结构无关的注意力分数启发式方法，无法保留几何上有价值的token。

Method: GHOST提出三个相互强化的创新：分层双级重要性评分方案、保护特殊token不被驱逐的特权机制、以及余弦相似度引导的逐层预算分配。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，GHOST在保持优秀重建质量的同时，将KV缓存削减近一半，推理速度相比最先进方法提升1.75倍。

Conclusion: GHOST通过利用模型自身3D几何输出进行在线token驱逐，有效解决了流式3D重建中的内存瓶颈问题，实现了效率与质量的双赢。

Abstract: Streaming 3D reconstruction from long monocular video sequences requires maintaining a key-value (KV) cache that grows linearly with sequence length, creating a severe memory bottleneck. Existing approaches either truncate the cache to a fixed set of anchor frames, leading to reconstruction quality degradation, or rely on attention-score heuristics that are agnostic to 3D scene structure, failing to preserve geometrically valuable tokens. To address these problems, we present GHOST (Geometry-Hierarchical Online Streaming Token Eviction), a training-free KV cache management framework that exploits the model's own 3D geometry outputs to evict redundant tokens online. GHOST introduces three mutually reinforcing innovations: a hierarchical dual-level importance scoring scheme, a privilege mechanism that protects special tokens from eviction, and a cosine-similarity-guided layer-wise budget allocation. Experiments on various benchmarks show that GHOST preserves excellent reconstruction quality while cutting the KV cache by nearly half and delivering 1.75x faster inference compared to state-of-the-art methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/lokiniuniu/GHOST.

</details>


### [54] [Do Less, Achieve More: Do We Need Every-Step Optimization for RL Fine-tuning of Diffusion Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15855)
*Renye Yan,Jikang Cheng,Shikun Sun,Yi Sun,You Wu,Wei Peng,Zongwei Wang,Ling Liang,Junliang Xing,Yimao Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaScope，一种自适应识别扩散模型去噪过程中最优RL干预时机并动态终止训练的插件，在提升生成质量的同时降低计算成本，实现性能提升66%和计算成本降低59%的"双重收益"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在RL微调时，对完整去噪轨迹施加RL存在两个关键问题：早期阶段动作-奖励不匹配导致高方差和低效更新，后期阶段奖励饱和导致过拟合局部细节和奖励破解。两个阶段的问题使得"做得多却收效少"，亟需一种自适应干预策略来解决延迟奖励和奖励饱和的双重困境。

Method: AdaScope通过感知去噪过程中的结构演化和语义一致性，自适应地识别最优RL干预时机（避开早期不稳定阶段和后期饱和阶段），并在去噪收敛和奖励增益饱和时动态终止训练，从而仅在关键阶段执行有效的RL微调。

Result: 相比现有最先进方法，AdaScope在提升生成性能66%的同时，将计算成本降低59%，实现了性能与效率的双赢。

Conclusion: AdaScope验证了扩散模型RL微调中"并非所有去噪阶段都值得投入RL"的核心观点，通过自适应时机选择打破了传统全轨迹RL的"投入产出悖论"，为高效偏好对齐提供了新范式。

Abstract: Despite strong image-generation performance, diffusion models' reconstruction objectives limit alignment with human preferences. RL enables such alignment through explicit rewards. However, most studies apply RL to the full denoising trajectory, making it computationally costly and weakening preference alignment, i.e., doing more but achieving less. We observe that the impact of RL fine-tuning varies significantly across denoising stages. In the early stage, image structures are unstable and distant from the final reward signal. Applying RL at this stage leads to delayed rewards and action-reward mismatching, resulting in high variance and inefficient updates. Conversely, in the later stage, reward gains saturate, and continued training tends to overfit local details, intensifying reward hacking. To tackle these challenges, we propose AdaScope, an RL-enhanced plug-in that improves generation quality while reducing computational cost. Specifically, AdaScope adaptively identifies the optimal intervention timing for RL by perceiving the structural evolution and semantic consistency during denoising, and dynamically terminates training once the denoising converges and reward gains saturate. As a result, it achieves a rare 'dual benefit': a reduction in computational costs alongside a significant performance improvement. We offer theoretical grounds for the design of AdaScope. Compared with state-of-the-art methods, AdaScope improves performance by 66% while cutting computational cost by 59%.

</details>


### [55] [On RGB-TIR Stereo Calibration under Extreme Resolution Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15860)
*Michał Król,Michał Salamonowicz,Władysław Skarbek,Michał Tomaszewski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对RGB相机（2028×1520 px）与极低分辨率热红外相机（80×62 px，像素比约1:625）组成的立体系统，提出了一种实用的标定框架。采用主动OLED屏幕动态切换棋盘格（TIR）和ChArUco（RGB）图案，结合专用角点检测算法和基线约束光束法平差，实现了32.7 mm基线（标称30 mm）和0.382像素的重投影误差，在建筑模型上验证了TIR到RGB投影的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB-热红外立体相机系统在多模态建筑围护结构分析中至关重要，但低成本热传感器空间分辨率极低，导致传统标定方法失效，亟需一种适用于极端分辨率差异的实用立体标定方案。

Method: 1) 使用主动OLED屏幕在同一物理表面动态切换不同模态的标定图案（TIR用棋盘格，RGB用ChArUco）；2) 设计专用角点检测算法：透视校正+Hessian鞍点分析+Mean Shift定位，实现80×62 px图像中的可靠棋盘格角点检测；3) 采用基线约束的光束法平差（Bundle Adjustment），在平面标定物退化条件下强制保持物理一致的刚体几何。

Result: 立体基线标定结果为32.7 mm（标称30 mm），整体重投影误差0.382像素。在热活性建筑模型上的恒定深度和逐像素深度估计实验中，验证了TIR到RGB投影的一致性。

Conclusion: 该标定框架有效解决了极低分辨率热红外相机与高分辨率RGB相机的立体标定难题，能够支持建筑能耗性能评估中的多模态分析应用。

Abstract: Accurate geometric calibration of RGB-thermal infrared (TIR) stereo camera systems is essential for multimodal building envelope analysis, yet remains challenging when low-cost thermal sensors with very low spatial resolution are employed. This paper presents a practical stereo calibration framework for an RGB camera (2028 x 1520 px) paired with a TIR camera operating at only 80 x 62 px - a pixel-count ratio of approximately 1:625. An active OLED screen dynamically switches modality-specific patterns (checkerboard for TIR, ChArUco for RGB) on a single physical surface, providing controlled and repeatable thermal contrast. A dedicated corner detection algorithm combining perspective rectification, Hessian saddle-point analysis, and Mean Shift localisation achieves reliable checkerboard detection at 80 x 62 px without per-frame parameter tuning. A baseline-constrained bundle adjustment enforces physically consistent rig geometry under the planar-calibration-object degeneracy, yielding a stereo baseline of 32.7 mm (nominal 30 mm) with an overall reprojection error of 0.382 px. The system is validated on a thermally active building mock-up using constant-depth and per-pixel depth estimation, demonstrating consistent TIR-to-RGB projection suitable for building energy performance assessment.

</details>


### [56] [SOLAR: Self-supervised Joint Learning for Symmetric Multimodal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15868)
*Wenjie Yang,Hang Yu,Yuyu Guo,Peng Di*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SOLAR框架，一种两阶段自监督方法，利用无标注网页图文对解决对称多模态到多模态（MM2MM）检索问题。通过第一阶段学习图文对的交集掩码对齐语义、保留差异，第二阶段利用掩码构造正例和难负例进行自监督嵌入学习。在构建的高质量人工验证基准上，SOLAR超越最强监督VLM 7.08个点，且参数量减少50倍以上，嵌入维度缩小5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称多模态到多模态（MM2MM）检索中查询和上下文可互换，但现有通用多模态检索方法受限于有标注的非对称数据集，无法有效处理这一任务。本文旨在解决这一关键但尚未充分探索的挑战。

Method: 两阶段自监督框架：第一阶段学习图文对的交集掩码，对齐跨模态共有语义同时保留各自差异信息；第二阶段利用学到的掩码构造正样本和难负样本，通过掩码图像/文本的不同部分进行自监督多模态嵌入学习。

Result: 在构建的高质量人工验证基准上，SOLAR超越最强监督VLM 7.08个点，参数量减少50倍以上，嵌入维度缩小5倍。

Conclusion: SOLAR有效解决了对称MM2MM检索问题，无需标注数据即可达到优于强监督方法的性能，展示了自监督学习在此任务上的巨大潜力。

Abstract: In this work, we address the critical yet underexplored challenge of symmetric multimodal-to-multimodal (MM2MM) retrieval, where queries and contexts are interchangeable. Existing universal multimodal retrieval works struggle with this task, as they are constrained by the labeled asymmetric datasets used. We produce SOLAR (Self-supervised jOint LeArning for symmetric multimodal Retrieval), a novel two-stage self-supervised framework that leverages readily available unlabeled web-scale image-text pairs. Based on the observation that both semantic alignment and discrepancies exist between two modalities, in the first stage, we learn the intersection mask of image-text pair, allowing us to align intersection while preserving semantic of difference. In the second stage, the learned mask is further utilized to construct positive and hardnegative samples via masking different parts of image/text, which enable us to conduct self-supervised multimodal embedding learning. Complementing this framework, we present a new benchmark featuring high-quality human-verified positive and hard-negative pairs to evaluate symmetric MM2MM retrieval under realistic conditions, as well as the corresponding pipeline. Extensive experiments against ten SOTA methods show SOLAR surpasses the strongest supervised VLM by 7.08 points on this benchmark, with over 50x fewer model parameters and a 5x smaller embedding dimension. Code and benchmark will be available soon.

</details>


### [57] [Unlocking Dense Metric Depth Estimation in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15876)
*Hanxun Yu,Xuan Qu,Yuxin Wang,Jianke Zhu,Lei ke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DepthVLM将单个VLM转化为原生稠密几何预测器，通过轻量级深度头与两阶段训练，在单次前向传播中同时生成全分辨率深度图和语言输出，显著优于现有VLM和纯视觉模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在3D理解上受限，文本-only监督范式无法约束细粒度视觉感知且无法恢复稠密几何；外部视觉模型蒸馏几何会导致误差累积，直接预测方法效率低或输出粗糙。

Method: 在LLM骨干上附加轻量级深度头，采用统一视觉-文本监督范式与两阶段训练策略，单次前向传播同时生成全分辨率深度图和语言输出。

Result: DepthVLM显著优于现有VLM且推理效率更高，超越领先的纯视觉模型，提升了复杂3D空间推理能力。

Conclusion: DepthVLM通过统一框架实现VLM原生稠密几何预测，迈向真正的统一基础模型。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) excel at 2D tasks such as grounding and captioning, yet remain limited in 3D understanding. A key limitation is their text-only supervision paradigm, which under-constrains fine-grained visual perception and prevents the recovery of dense geometry. Prior methods either distill geometry from external vision models, introducing error accumulation, or enable direct prediction with inefficient per-pixel query or coarse token-level outputs. In this paper, we propose DepthVLM, a simple yet effective framework that transforms a single VLM into a native dense geometry predictor while preserving its multimodal capability. By attaching a lightweight depth head to the LLM backbone and training under a unified vision-text supervision paradigm with a two-stage schedule, DepthVLM generates full-resolution depth maps alongside language outputs in a single forward pass. We further introduce a unified indoor-outdoor metric depth benchmark in a VLM-compatible format. Experiments show that DepthVLM significantly outperforms existing VLMs with higher inference efficiency, surpasses leading pure vision models, and improves complex 3D spatial reasoning, moving toward a truly unified foundation model. All code and checkpoints will be publicly released.

</details>


### [58] [FSCM: Frequency-Enhanced Spatial-Spectral Coupled Mamba for Infrared Hyperspectral Image Colorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15880)
*Tingting Liu,Yuan Liu,Guiping Chen,Xiubao Sui,Qian Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FSCM，一种基于光谱信息引导的GAN框架，用于红外图像彩色化。该框架包含频率增强的空间-光谱状态空间生成器（由级联FSB单元组成），每个FSB集成状态空间建模、频率增强模块和双流混合门控模块，并引入在线语义分割引导损失，在视觉质量和语义保真度上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红外彩色化方法主要依赖单波段图像，光谱线索不足导致结构失真和语义混淆；尽管高光谱图像提供丰富光谱信息，但现有单波段框架难以建模空间-光谱耦合和弱纹理细节。

Method: 提出FSCM框架：(1) 频率增强的空间-光谱状态空间生成器，由级联FSB单元组成；(2) 每个FSB集成状态空间建模（捕获全局空间-光谱依赖）、频率增强模块FEM（多级小波分解+傅里叶门控恢复结构轮廓与高频细节）、双流混合门控模块DGM（变形感知采样+稀疏注意力增强局部结构抑制背景干扰）；(3) 在线语义分割引导损失约束结果语义一致性。

Result: 实验表明FSCM在视觉质量和语义保真度上优于现有红外彩色化方法。

Conclusion: FSCM通过光谱信息引导和频率增强的空间-光谱状态空间建模，有效解决了红外图像彩色化中的结构失真与语义混淆问题，适用于全天候感知场景。

Abstract: Thermal infrared imaging is robust to illumination variations and smoke interference, making it important for all-weather perception. However, the lack of natural color and fine texture limits target recognition, human visual interpretation, and the transfer of visible-light models. Existing infrared colorization methods mainly rely on single-band images, where insufficient spectral cues may lead to structural distortion and semantic confusion. Although infrared hyperspectral images provide rich spectral responses and material information, existing single-band frameworks remain limited in modeling spatial-spectral coupling and weak texture details. To address these issues, this paper presents FSCM, a spectral-information-guided GAN framework. Within FSCM, a frequency-enhanced spatial-spectral state-space generator composed of cascaded FSB units is constructed. Each FSB integrates three complementary components: state-space modeling captures global spatial-spectral dependencies; the frequency enhancement module (FEM) combines multi-level wavelet decomposition and Fourier gating to recover structural contours, directional high-frequency details, and global frequency responses; and the dual-stream hybrid gating module (DGM) integrates deformation-aware sampling with sparse attention to enhance effective local structures and suppress background interference. Additionally, an online semantic segmentation-guided loss is introduced to constrain the generated results, improving semantic consistency in complex road scenes. Experiments show that FSCM outperforms existing infrared colorization methods in visual quality and semantic fidelity.

</details>


### [59] [A Causally Grounded Taxonomy for Image Degradation Robustness Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15906)
*Stefan Becker,Simon Weiss,Wolfgang Hübner,Michael Arens*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于因果关系的图像退化组织框架，通过双轴分类（因果来源+感知效果）和轻量级严重性度量层（PSNR/SSIM/LPIPS），在无需修改现有基准的前提下统一不同社区对图像退化的研究，并构建了COCO Degradation基准验证目标检测器鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像退化研究分散在多个社区（合成退化基准、感知质量评估、物理成像系统分析），使用不兼容的分组方案和严重性定义，导致结果难以跨数据集、退化源和任务进行比较。

Method: 提出因果驱动的双轴分类框架：(1) 因果来源轴：环境、传感器/光学、ISP/渲染器/编解码器、传输/系统；(2) 感知效果轴。引入轻量级严重性度量层，使用PSNR、SSIM、LPIPS量化退化强度，使不同后端的严重性可观察和可比较。

Result: 构建了COCO Degradation基准，这是一个与分类法对齐的基准，用于评估目标检测器在多样成像条件下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架不引入新退化或重新定义现有基准，而是提供解释性表示和度量层，使隐含假设显式化，实现跨数据集的统一比较。

Abstract: Image degradations can occur during acquisition, processing, and transmission, altering visual appearance and affecting downstream vision tasks. They are studied in several communities, including synthetic corruption benchmarks for robustness evaluation, perceptual image quality assessment, and physically grounded analyses of imaging systems or real camera failures. Although these areas address closely related phenomena, they often use incompatible grouping schemes and backend specific severity definitions, making results difficult to compare across datasets, degradation sources, and tasks.
  We propose a causally grounded framework for organizing and interpreting image degradations across these settings. Instead of introducing new degradations or redefining existing benchmarks, we provide an interpretive representation and measurement layer that makes implicit assumptions explicit. Each degradation is described along two orthogonal axes: its dominant causal source in the imaging pipeline (environment, sensor/optics, ISP/renderer/codec, or transfer/system), and its resulting perceptual effect. This dual axis abstraction yields a compact taxonomy spanning algorithmic corruptions, perceptual distortions, and physically motivated imaging artifacts.
  To address inconsistent severity semantics without changing existing implementations, we introduce a lightweight severity measurement layer. For every degradation and each native severity level of a given backend, we quantify degradation strength using full reference image quality metrics: PSNR, SSIM, and LPIPS. This makes severity observable and comparable across sources while preserving native parameterizations. We demonstrate the framework through COCO Degradation, a taxonomy aligned benchmark for evaluating object detector robustness under diverse imaging conditions.

</details>


### [60] [RaPD: Resolution-Agnostic Pixel Diffusion via Semantics-Enriched Implicit Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15908)
*Yanhao Ge,Shanyan Guan,Weihao Wang,Ying Tai,Mingyu You*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RaPD，在连续神经图像场（NIF）潜空间中进行扩散，实现分辨率无关的图像生成，通过语义表示引导和坐标查询注意力渲染器弥合重建与生成鸿沟，仅需改变查询坐标即可任意分辨率渲染，扩散成本固定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然图像是连续的，但现有生成模型通常在离散网格上合成图像，限制了分辨率灵活生成。先前方法仅在解码阶段引入连续性作为插值模块，生成潜空间仍是离散且面向重建的。

Method: 提出RaPD（Resolution-agnostic Pixel Diffusion），在连续神经图像场（NIF）潜空间中进行扩散；采用语义表示指导（Semantic Representation Guidance）进行生成感知的潜空间学习；设计坐标查询注意力渲染器（Coordinate-Queried Attention Renderer）实现坐标条件化和尺度感知渲染。

Result: 实验表明，所提方法在生成质量和分辨率可扩展性上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: RaPD通过将扩散过程置于连续潜空间，实现了真正的分辨率无关生成，且单个去噪潜变量可通过改变查询坐标在任意分辨率下渲染，扩散计算成本保持固定。

Abstract: Natural images are continuous, yet most generative models synthesize them on discrete grids, limiting resolution-flexible generation. Continuous neural fields enable resolution-free rendering, but prior methods introduce continuity only at the decoding stage as an interpolation module, leaving the generative latent space discretized and reconstruction-oriented. We propose RaPD (Resolution-agnostic Pixel Diffusion), which performs diffusion in a continuous Neural Image Field (NIF) latent space. RaPD bridges this reconstruction-generation gap with Semantic Representation Guidance for generation-aware latent learning and a Coordinate-Queried Attention Renderer for coordinate-conditioned, scale-aware rendering. A single denoised latent can be rendered at arbitrary resolutions by changing only the query coordinates, keeping diffusion cost fixed. Experiments demonstrate superior generation quality and resolution scalability.

</details>


### [61] [AdaEraser: Training-Free Object Removal via Adaptive Attention Suppression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15921)
*Dingming Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AdaEraser，一种自适应注意力抑制框架，通过逐token动态调节自注意力强度，在无需训练的情况下实现优于训练方法的物体移除效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无训练方法在物体移除时对空缺区域进行无差别自注意力抑制，导致模型需同时重建背景内容，生成质量下降。需要一种能区分目标物体与背景的精细注意力调控策略。

Method: 通过分析去噪过程中自注意力图的演化，设计逐token自适应注意力抑制策略：根据目标物体概念的估计存在度，动态调整自注意力层的抑制强度，实现渐进式感知物体移除。

Result: 实验表明AdaEraser在物体移除任务上达到优越性能，甚至超越基于训练的方法。

Conclusion: 自适应注意力抑制有效解决了无训练物体移除中背景重建与物体消除的冲突，为高质量图像编辑提供了新范式。

Abstract: Object removal aims to eliminate specified objects from images while plausibly inpainting the affected regions with background content. Current training-free methods typically block attention to object regions within self-attention layers during the image generation process, leveraging surrounding background information to restore the image. However, indiscriminate suppression of self-attention in the vacated areas can degrade generation quality, as the model must simultaneously reconstruct background content in these regions. To solve this conflict, we propose AdaEraser, an adaptive framework that dynamically modulates attention based on the estimated presence of target object concepts. Through analysis of self-attention map evolution across denoising timesteps before and during removal, we develop a token-wise adaptive attention suppression strategy. This approach enables progressive perception of object removal throughout the denoising process, with the suppression strength in self-attention layers adjusted adaptively. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AdaEraser achieves superior performance in object removal, outperforming even training-based methods.

</details>


### [62] [Invaria: Learning Scale and Density Invariance in Point Clouds via Next-Resolution Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15923)
*Chun-Peng Chang,Shaoxiang Wang,Alain Pagani,Dariu Gavrila,Holger Caesar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Invaria，一种通过下一分辨率预测和感受野校准实现尺度与密度不变性的3D点云编码器，在分辨率变化和尺度缩放下显著提升性能，同时模型尺寸缩小45%，输入token减少40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D点云编码器对采样分辨率和尺度变化高度敏感，导致性能显著下降，成为机器人等真实场景部署的主要瓶颈。作者旨在解决这一过拟合问题，使模型学习尺度与密度不变的语义特征。

Method: 提出Invaria编码器，核心包括两个技术：1) 下一分辨率预测（next-resolution prediction）作为训练目标；2) 感受野校准（receptive field calibration）。该方法不旨在显式生成高分辨率点云，而是通过该训练目标促使模型学习鲁棒的结构不变性。

Result: 在ScanNet上，3倍低分辨率下mIoU提升56.0%，物体尺度缩小3倍时性能提升20%。模型尺寸缩小45%，输入token平均减少40%。

Conclusion: 下一分辨率预测和感受野校准能有效解耦3D点云的语义信息与分辨率/尺度，实现高效且鲁棒的表示学习，为3D领域实现类似2D图像编码器的泛化能力提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Modern image encoders achieve high generalization by decoupling semantic meaning from resolution, an ability yet to be fully realized in the 3D domain. We investigate the failure of 3D point cloud encoders to achieve similar generalization and find that existing models are highly sensitive to sampling resolution and scale changes, leading to significant performance degradation. This sensitivity is a major bottleneck for real-world deployment in robotics, as it suggests models overfit to specific quantization densities and object scales rather than learning invariant semantic features. To mitigate this dependency, we propose Invaria, a point cloud encoder that achieves scale and density invariance through next-resolution prediction and receptive field calibration. While our objective is not the explicit generation of high-resolution point clouds, we find that this training objective encourages the model to learn robust, structural invariants. The resulting encoder achieves significant performance gains during resolution shifts while maintaining high efficiency through a compact model size and reduced token requirements. Specifically, on ScanNet, Invaria achieves a 56.0\% higher mIoU at 3$\times$ lower resolution and a 20\% improvement when the objects scale is reduced by a factor of 3. These gains are achieved with a 45\% smaller model size and an average reduction of 40\% in input tokens.

</details>


### [63] [Decomposed Vision-Language Alignment for Fine-Grained Open-Vocabulary Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15942)
*Chenhao Wang,Yingrui Ji,Yu Meng,Yao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分解式视觉-语言对齐框架，通过将文本提示显式分解为概念token和属性token，并利用特征门控交叉注意力模块和log空间相似度聚合，提升开放词汇分割模型在未见过的属性-类别组合上的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇分割模型在处理细粒度描述时，将多个语义单元编码为整体句子，导致模型难以泛化到未见过的类别与属性组合。

Method: 1) 将文本提示分解为概念token和多个属性token，实现每个语义单元的独立跨模态交互；2) 提出特征门控交叉注意力模块，生成属性特定的门控图以乘法方式融合特征；3) 在评分层面，对每个token的相似度在log空间进行聚合，实现稳定可解释的组合匹配。

Result: 该方法可无缝集成到现有基于Transformer的分割架构中，在细粒度开放词汇分割基准上显著提升了对未见属性-类别组合的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过显式分解文本语义单元并分别进行跨模态交互，有效解决了细粒度开放词汇分割中的组合泛化问题，且具有良好的可集成性。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary segmentation models often struggle to generalize to unseen combinations of object categories and attributes, because fine-grained descriptions are typically encoded as holistic sentences that entangle multiple semantic units. We propose a Decomposed Vision-Language Alignment framework that explicitly factorizes textual prompts into a concept token and multiple attribute tokens, enabling separate cross-modal interactions for each semantic unit. At the feature level, we introduce a Feature-Gated Cross-Attention module that generates attribute-specific gating maps to fuse information in a multiplicative manner, effectively enforcing compositional semantics. At the scoring level, per-token similarities are aggregated in log-space, producing a stable and interpretable compositional matching. The method can be seamlessly integrated into existing transformer-based segmentation architectures and significantly improves generalization to unseen attribute-category compositions in fine-grained open-vocabulary segmentation benchmarks.

</details>


### [64] [From Failure to Feedback: Group Revision Unlocks Hard Cases in Object-Level Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15951)
*Yuyuan Liu,Yiping Ji,Anjie Le,Jiayuan Zhu,Jiazhen Pan,Can Peng,Jiajun Deng,Fengbei Liu,Junde Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GroupRevision方法，通过组修订优化范式增强视觉-语言模型在困难样本上的对象级定位能力，相比传统GRPO方法在多个基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GRPO的强化学习方法仅在响应级别分配奖励，这种稀疏奖励机制在困难场景下（所有候选响应均失败时）提供极少的有效学习信号，限制了模型在挑战性案例上的学习效果。

Method: 提出组修订优化范式：1) 采样初始响应并生成一组修订候选以探索更优定位结果；2) 受奖励塑造启发，引入整合过程量化每个候选相较初始尝试的提升，并将其转化为信息性塑造信号；3) 利用这些信号优化奖励并调节优势值，放大高质量修订的影响。

Result: 在指代分割、推理分割、REC和计数基准上相比先前基于GRPO的模型取得一致性能提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的组修订优化范式有效解决了GRPO在困难样本上奖励稀疏的问题，通过密集化学习信号显著提升了大型视觉-语言模型在对象级定位任务上的表现。

Abstract: Finetuning Large Vision-Language Models with reinforcement learning has emerged as a promising approach to enhance their capability in object-level grounding. However, existing methods, mainly based on GRPO, assign rewards at the response level. Such sparse reward, often criterion-induced, leads to minimal learning signals when all candidate responses fail in challenging scenarios. In this work, we propose a group-revision optimisation paradigm that enhances learning on hard cases. It begins with a sampled initial response and generates a set of revised candidates to explore improved grounding outcomes. Inspired by reward shaping, we introduce a consolidation process that quantifies each candidate's improvement over the initial attempt and converts it into informative shaping signals. These signals are used to both refine the reward and modulate the advantage, amplifying the influence of high-quality revisions. Our method achieves consistent gains across referring and reasoning segmentation, REC, and counting benchmarks compared with prior GRPO-based models. Our code is available at https://github.com/yyliu01/GroupRevision.

</details>


### [65] [Sparse Autoencoders enable Robust and Interpretable Fine-tuning of CLIP models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15961)
*Fabian Morelli,Arnas Uselis,Ankit Sonthalia,Seong Joon Oh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SAE-FT方法，通过在预训练视觉语言模型的视觉表征上训练稀疏自编码器（SAE）来识别语义特征，并在微调过程中正则化这些特征的变化，从而在保持分布外鲁棒性的同时提升下游任务性能，无需昂贵文本引导，计算高效且可解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练视觉语言模型（如CLIP）在下游任务微调时往往牺牲对分布偏移的鲁棒性，现有方法虽试图缓解这一权衡但依赖计算昂贵的文本引导。本文旨在提出一种仅操作于视觉表征的高效鲁棒微调方法。

Method: 在预训练模型的视觉表征上训练稀疏自编码器（SAE），提取语义上有意义的特征；在微调过程中，通过惩罚这些特征的添加和移除来正则化表征变化，从而防止灾难性遗忘并实现可解释的语义变化分析。

Result: 在ImageNet及其分布偏移基准测试上，SAE-FT匹配或超越了现有最先进方法的性能，同时具有计算高效性和机制透明性。

Conclusion: 基于视觉表征的稀疏自编码器正则化能有效平衡下游任务性能与分布鲁棒性，为鲁棒微调提供了一种无需文本引导、可解释且计算高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Large-scale pre-trained vision-language models like CLIP demonstrate remarkable zero-shot performance across diverse tasks. However, fine-tuning these models to improve downstream performance often degrades robustness against distribution shifts. Recent approaches have attempted to mitigate this trade-off, but often rely on computationally expensive text-guidance. We propose a novel method for robust fine-tuning, SAE-FT, which operates only on the model's visual representations. SAE-FT regularizes changes to these representations by penalizing the addition and removal of semantically meaningful features identified by a Sparse Autoencoder trained on the pre-trained model. This constraint prevents catastrophic forgetting and makes the fine-tuning process interpretable, enabling direct analysis of semantic changes. SAE-FT is both mechanistically transparent and computationally efficient, matching or exceeding state-of-the-art performance on ImageNet and its associated distribution shift benchmarks. Code is publicly available at: https://github.com/Fabian-Mor/sae-ft.

</details>


### [66] [Flash-GRPO: Efficient Alignment for Video Diffusion via One-Step Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15980)
*Xiaoxuan He,Siming Fu,Zeyue Xue,Weijie Wang,Ruizhe He,Yuming Li,Dacheng Yin,Shuai Dong,Haoyang Huang,Hongfa Wang,Nan Duan,Bohan Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Flash-GRPO提出了一种单步训练框架，通过等时分组和时序梯度修正，在低计算预算下实现比全轨迹训练更优的对齐质量，并显著提升训练效率，有效解决了GRPO在视频扩散模型对齐中的计算瓶颈和不稳定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO在视频扩散模型对齐中面临严重的计算瓶颈（14B模型需数百GPU天），现有滑动窗口子采样方法牺牲优化质量，导致不稳定且无法达到全轨迹性能，亟需一种高效且高质量的训练框架。

Method: 提出Flash-GRPO单步训练框架，包含两个关键技术：1) 等时分组（Iso-temporal Grouping），通过强制提示维度的时间一致性消除时间步混杂方差；2) 时序梯度修正（Temporal Gradient Rectification），中和时间依赖的缩放因子以解决不同时间步梯度幅度不一致的问题。

Result: 在1.3B到14B参数模型上的实验验证了Flash-GRPO的有效性，实现了显著的训练加速、一致的稳定性以及最先进的对齐质量。

Conclusion: Flash-GRPO在低计算预算下能够超越全轨迹训练的对齐质量，同时大幅提升训练效率，为解决大规模视频扩散模型对齐的计算效率问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization has emerged as essential for aligning video diffusion models with human preferences, but faces a critical computational bottleneck: training a 14B parametered model typically demands hundreds of GPU days per experiment. Existing efficiency methods reduce costs through sliding window subsampling training timesteps, but fundamentally compromise optimization, exhibiting severe instability and failing to reach full trajectory performance. We present Flash-GRPO, a single-step training framework that outperforms full trajectory training in alignment quality under low computational budgets while substantially improving training efficiency. Flash-GRPO addresses two critical challenges: iso-temporal grouping eliminates timestep-confounded variance by enforcing prompt-wise temporal consistency, decoupling policy performance from timestep difficulty; temporal gradient rectification neutralizes the time-dependent scaling factor that causes vastly inconsistent gradient magnitudes across timesteps. Experiments on 1.3B to 14B parameter models validate Flash-GRPO's effectiveness, demonstrating substantial training acceleration with consistent stability and state-of-the-art alignment quality.

</details>


### [67] [Echo-Forcing: A Scene Memory Framework for Interactive Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16003)
*Mingqiang Wu,Weilun Feng,Zhefeng Zhang,Haotong Qin,Yuqi Li,Guoxin Fan,Xiaokun Liu,Zhulin An,Libo Huang,Yongjun Xu,Chuanguang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Echo-Forcing 提出了一种无需训练的场景记忆框架，通过解耦历史KV状态中的稳定锚点与近期动态，解决了自回归视频扩散模型在交互式长视频生成中的场景遗忘、提示响应延迟和长程记忆丢失问题。该方法在VBench-Long基准上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练-free的长视频优化方法主要面向单提示下的稳定扩展，难以处理交互式场景中的提示切换、旧场景遗忘和历史场景召回。核心瓶颈在于历史KV状态的功能纠缠：稳定锚点与近期动态由同一缓存策略处理，导致背景污染、新提示响应延迟和长程记忆丢失。

Method: 提出Echo-Forcing框架，包含三个核心机制：(1) 层次化时序记忆，在相对RoPE下解耦稳定锚点、压缩历史和近期窗口；(2) 场景召回帧，将历史场景压缩为空间结构化的KV表征以支持长程召回；(3) 差异感知记忆衰减，根据新旧场景差异自适应遗忘冲突令牌。该框架在有界缓存预算下统一支持平滑过渡、硬切变和长程场景召回。

Result: 在VBench-Long基准上的广泛评估表明，Echo-Forcing在长视频生成和交互式视频生成设置中均取得了最佳整体性能。

Conclusion: Echo-Forcing通过解耦历史KV状态的功能角色，在无需额外训练的条件下有效解决了交互式长视频生成中的关键挑战，为自回归视频扩散模型在交互场景中的应用提供了有效的记忆管理方案。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models enable open-ended generation through local attention and KV caching. However, existing training-free long-video optimization methods mainly focus on stable extension under a single prompt, making them difficult to handle interactive scenarios involving prompt switching, old scene forgetting, and historical scene recall. We identify the core bottleneck as the functional entanglement of historical KV states: stable anchors and recent dynamics are handled by the same cache policy, leading to outdated background contamination, delayed response to new prompts, and loss of long-range memory. To address this issue, we propose Echo-Forcing, a training-free scene memory framework specifically designed for interactive long video generation with three core mechanisms: (1) Hierarchical Temporal Memory, which decouples stable anchors, compressed history, and recent windows under relative RoPE; (2) Scene Recall Frames, which compresses historical scenes into spatially structured KV representations to support long-term recall; and (3) Difference-aware Memory Decay, which adaptively forgets conflicting tokens according to the discrepancy between old and new scenes. Based on these designs, Echo-Forcing uniformly supports smooth transitions, hard cuts, and long-range scene recall under a bounded cache budget. Extensive evaluations on VBench-Long further demonstrate that Echo-Forcing achieves the best overall performance in both long-video generation and interactive video generation settings. Our code is released in https://github.com/mingqiangWu/Echo-Forcing

</details>


### [68] [End-to-end plaque counting and virus titration from laboratory plate images with deep learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16008)
*Eugenia Moris,Alicia Costábile,Sebastián Rey,Irene Ferreiro,Joaquín Hurtado,Lizandra Lissette Luciano,Matías Villagrán,Aisha Espino Vázquez,Jomari Ramos,Isadora Monteiro,María Victoria de Santiago,Pilar Moreno,Gonzalo Moratorio,José Ignacio Orlando*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于SAM/SAM2模型的端到端计算机辅助工作流，用于自动从病毒空斑实验图像中检测和计数空斑，并计算空斑形成单位（PFU/mL）。该方法在三种病毒数据集上验证，与人工标注的Pearson相关系数达0.88-0.92，显著减少人工标注工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统空斑实验依赖人工计数，耗时且存在操作者间差异。为了解决这一瓶颈，研究者希望开发一种自动化、可复现、可扩展的计算机辅助工作流，实现从原始图像到最终病毒滴度报告的全流程自动化。

Method: 提出两阶段分割流水线：(1) SAM2-based 孔分割模块，在不同成像条件下定位检测孔；(2) SAM-based 空斑分割模型，在孔内检测并计数空斑。最终自动计算每孔空斑数和PFU/mL，并集成到web平台供用户审查和组织实验。

Result: 在MAYV/CVB3图像（17张）上Pearson相关系数为0.92，在VACV图像（22张）上为0.88。与四位独立专家的标注结果高度一致。方法在6孔板和12孔板两种格式上均表现良好。

Conclusion: 该自动化工作流可显著减少人工标注工作量，提供可复现、可扩展、可审计的空斑实验分析方案，代码将在论文被接收后开源发布。

Abstract: Plaque assays remain the gold standard readout of virus infectivity; however, plaque counting from plate images is labor-intensive and prone to inter-operator variability. We present an end-to-end, computer-aided workflow for cytopathic effect-based virus titration directly from laboratory plaque assay images. The proposed approach combines two models derived from the Segment Anything Model (SAM): a SAM2-based well-segmentation module that localizes assay wells across heterogeneous imaging conditions, and a SAM-based plaque-segmentation model that detects and enumerates plaques within each well. The method was evaluated on a mixed dataset comprising private plaque assay images of Mayaro virus and Coxsackievirus B3, together with public Vaccinia virus images from the VACVPlaque dataset. The pipeline outputs per-well plaque counts, automatically computes plaque-forming units per milliliter (PFU/mL), and is integrated into a web-based platform that allows users to review results and organize experiments. On held-out plates (17 from MAYV/CVB3 and 22 from VACV), the workflow generalized across two plate formats (6-well and 12-well) and showed strong agreement with manual annotations (Pearson correlation coefficients of 0.92 for MAYV/CVB3 and 0.88 for VACV). Automated plaque counts were further compared with annotations from four independent experts, demonstrating high concordance. The proposed system will be open sourced and publicly released upon acceptance of this manuscript to enable reproducible, scalable, and audit-ready plaque assay analysis while substantially reducing manual annotation effort.

</details>


### [69] [EndoGSim: Physics-Aware 4D Dynamic Endoscopic Scene Simulations via MLLM-Guided Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16022)
*Changjing Liu,Yiming Huang,Long Bai,Beilei Cui,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个统一框架，利用多模态大语言模型引导的高斯泼溅法，实现内窥镜场景的物理感知重建与真实物理模拟，在开源和内部数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有内窥镜场景重建方法主要关注视觉重建，缺乏基于物理的场景描述，无法支持真实感模拟，限制了机器人辅助微创手术中下游任务的提升。

Method: 采用4D高斯泼溅（4DGS）集成预训练分割和深度估计来表示可变形组织和工具；引入对象级材料场，通过MLLM初始化材料参数，并利用可微分的物质点法（MPM）在渲染图像和光流联合监督下优化材料参数。

Result: 在开源和内部数据集上，该框架在模拟保真度和物理准确性方面均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了推动机器人辅助手术应用的潜力，为内窥镜场景提供了物理感知重建与真实物理模拟的统一方案。

Abstract: In robot-assisted minimally invasive surgery, high-fidelity dynamic endoscopic scene reconstruction and simulation are crucial to enhancing downstream tasks and advancing surgical outcomes. However, existing methods primarily focus on visual reconstruction, lacking physics-based descriptions of the scene required for realistic simulation. We propose a unified framework that achieves physics-aware reconstruction and physical simulation of endoscopic scenes through Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs)-guided Gaussian Splatting. Our approach utilizes 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) integrated with pre-trained segmentation and depth estimation to represent deformable tissues and tools. To achieve automatic inference of physical properties, we introduce an object-wise material field that initializes material parameters via MLLM and refines them through a differentiable Material Point Method (MPM) under joint supervision from rendered images and optical flow. Validated on both open-source and in-house datasets, our framework achieves superior simulation fidelity and physical accuracy compared to state-of-the-art methods, underscoring its potential to advance robot-assisted surgical applications.

</details>


### [70] [Robust Prior-Guided Segmentation for Editable 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16065)
*Raushan Joshi,Jean-Yves Guillemaut*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合SAM-HQ与先验引导标签重分配的方法，提升3D高斯泼溅的语义分割精度，支持交互式实时物体编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D-GS缺乏鲁棒分割能力，2D分割提升至3D时存在视图不一致和粗糙掩码问题，标准SAM在边界保真度和精细结构保留上存在局限。

Method: 利用SAM-HQ生成高精度2D掩码；引入先验引导的标签重分配方法，通过多视图一致性约束和先验学习为3D高斯分配标签。

Result: 达到最先进的分割精度，支持交互式实时物体编辑，边界保留效果优异，在VR和机器人场景中表现出实用性。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了3D分割中的视图不一致与边界模糊问题，推动了3D场景编辑在交互应用中的发展。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3D-GS) enables real-time 3D scene reconstruction but lacks robust segmentation for editing tasks such as object removal, extraction, and recoloring. Existing approaches that lift 2D segmentations to the 3D domain suffer from view inconsistencies and coarse masks. In this paper, we propose a novel framework that leverages the Segment Anything Model High Quality (SAM-HQ) to generate accurate 2D masks, addressing the limitations of the standard SAM in boundary fidelity and fine-structure preservation. To achieve robust 3D segmentation of any target object in a given scene, we introduce a prior-guided label reassignment method that assigns labels to 3D Gaussians by enforcing multiview consistency with learned priors. Our approach achieves state-of-the-art segmentation accuracy and enables interactive, real-time object editing while maintaining high visual fidelity. Qualitative results demonstrate superior boundary preservation and practical utility in Virtual Reality (VR) and robotics, advancing 3D scene editing.

</details>


### [71] [VideoSeeker: Incentivizing Instance-level Video Understanding via Native Agentic Tool Invocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16079)
*Yiming Zhao,Yu Zeng,Wenxuan Huang,Zhen Fang,Qing Miao,Qisheng Su,Jiawei Zhao,Jiayin Cai,Lin Chen,Zehui Chen,Yukun Qi,Yao Hu,Xiaolong Jiang,Feng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VideoSeeker，一种通过视觉提示实现实例级视频理解的新范式。它集成了智能推理与实例级视频理解任务，使模型能够主动感知并按需检索相关视频片段。通过四阶段全自动数据合成流水线生成大规模高质量数据，并利用冷启动监督和强化学习内化工具调用和主动感知能力。实验表明，该模型在实例级视频理解任务上平均提升+13.7%，超过GPT-4o和Gemini-2.5-Pro等闭源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLMs在需要精确时空定位的实例级视频理解任务中面临挑战。文本提示难以提供精确的空间和时间参考，导致用户体验差；同时当前方法将视觉感知与语言推理解耦，以语言而非视觉内容为中心进行推理，限制了模型主动感知细粒度视觉证据的能力。

Method: 1）提出VideoSeeker范式，通过视觉提示集成智能推理与实例级视频理解；2）构建四阶段全自动数据合成流水线，高效生成大规模高质量实例级视频数据；3）通过冷启动监督和强化学习（RL）训练，内化工具调用和主动感知能力。

Result: 在实例级视频理解任务上平均提升+13.7%，超越GPT-4o和Gemini-2.5-Pro等强大闭源模型，同时在通用视频理解基准上展现出有效的迁移能力。

Conclusion: VideoSeeker通过视觉提示和主动推理范式有效解决了实例级视频理解中时空定位的难题，证明了从文本为中心转向视觉为中心推理的优势，且方法具备良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have shown significant progress in video understanding, yet they face substantial challenges in tasks requiring precise spatiotemporal localization at the instance level. Existing methods primarily rely on text prompts for human-model interaction, but these prompts struggle to provide precise spatial and temporal references, resulting in poor user experience. Furthermore, current approaches typically decouple visual perception from language reasoning, centering reasoning around language rather than visual content, which limits the model's ability to proactively perceive fine-grained visual evidence. To address these challenges, we propose VideoSeeker, a novel paradigm for instance-level video understanding through visual prompts. VideoSeeker seamlessly integrates agentic reasoning with instance-level video understanding tasks, enabling the model to proactively perceive and retrieve relevant video segments on demand. We construct a four-stage fully automated data synthesis pipeline to efficiently generate large-scale, high-quality instance-level video data. We internalize tool-calling and proactive perception capabilities into the model via cold-start supervision and RL training, building a powerful video understanding model. Experiments demonstrate that our model achieves an average improvement of +13.7% over baselines on instance-level video understanding tasks, surpassing powerful closed-source models such as GPT-4o and Gemini-2.5-Pro, while also showing effective transferability on general video understanding benchmarks. The relevant datasets and code will be released publicly.

</details>


### [72] [ReAlign: Generalizable Image Forgery Detection via Reasoning-Aligned Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16080)
*Qing Huang,Zhipei Xu,Xuanyu Zhang,Xiangyu Yu,Jian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReAlign框架，通过GRPO优化的LLM生成高质量推理文本，经对比学习蒸馏到轻量级AIGI检测器中，在保持高效轻量的同时继承了语义泛化能力，在多项基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非LLM方法缺乏语义理解，LLM方法计算开销大且对细微伪影不敏感；此外，LLM生成的推理文本对检测性能的真正贡献尚不明确。本文旨在探索推理文本的内在价值，并将其高效融入轻量级检测器。

Method: (1) 使用GRPO优化的LLM生成高质量推理文本；(2) 通过对比学习将这些文本蒸馏到轻量级AIGI检测器中；(3) 采用联合优化策略，融合对比损失（图像-文本对齐）和分类损失（伪造判别）。

Result: 在AIGCDetectBenchmark、AIGI-Holmes及自建UltraSynth-10k数据集上，ReAlign在准确率和泛化能力上均一致优于现有SOTA方法，尤其面对高保真复杂伪造图像时表现出色。

Conclusion: LLM生成的推理文本蕴含语义泛化能力和语义级错误敏感性，通过蒸馏可有效增强轻量级检测器的性能，验证了推理文本对伪造检测的实质贡献。

Abstract: The rise of AI-generated images (AIGIs) poses growing challenges for digital authenticity, prompting the need for efficient, generalizable image forgery detection systems. Existing methods, whether non-LLM-based or LLM-based, exhibit distinct advantages and limitations. While non-LLM-based models offer efficient low-level artifact detection, they often lack semantic understanding. Conversely, LLM-based methods provide strong semantic reasoning and explainability but are computationally intensive and less sensitive to subtle visual artifacts. Moreover, the true contribution of explanatory reasoning texts to forgery detection performance remains unclear. In this work, we investigate the intrinsic value and potential of LLM-generated reasoning texts, considering it a source of generalization and semantic-error sensitivity. Based on these findings, we propose ReAlign, a novel framework that distills high-quality reasoning texts generated by a GRPO-optimized LLM into a lightweight AIGI detector via contrastive learning. ReAlign effectively inherits the generalization ability and semantic sensitivity capability of reasoning textual representations, while remaining efficient and lightweight for deployment. Moreover, ReAlign adopts a tailored joint optimization strategy that integrates contrastive loss for image-text alignment and classification loss for accurate forgery discrimination. Experimental results on AIGCDetectBenchmark, AIGI-Holmes, and our newly constructed UltraSynth-10k demonstrate that ReAlign consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art detectors in both accuracy and generalization, particularly when facing complex, high-fidelity forgeries from modern generative models.

</details>


### [73] [GenShield: Unified Detection and Artifact Correction for AI-Generated Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16122)
*Zhipei Xu,Xuanyu Zhang,Youmin Xu,Qing Huang,Shen Chen,Taiping Yao,Shouhong Ding,Jian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GenShield，一个统一的自回归框架，联合执行可解释的AIGI检测和可控伪影矫正，通过"诊断-修复"闭环实现AI生成图像的检测与修复，并引入基于视觉思维链的课程学习策略实现多步自解释矫正，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
